Annual Report of the Company and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) for       司（「本集團」）截至 2025 年 12 月 31 日止年度之
the year ended 31 December 2025.                                          年報。

CLOSING REMARKS                                                            結語
As a food manufacturer headquartered in Hong Kong, with operations         作為一家總部設於香港並於中國內地及其他地區
in Hong Kong, the Chinese Mainland and other regions’ markets, the         市場營運的食品製造商，本集團肩負支持當地社
Group bears the responsibility of supporting local communities and         區及其日常生活的責任。作為主席兼行政總裁，
their daily lives. As Chairman and CEO, I focus on strengthening our       本人專注於加強我們的產品競爭力（此為我們的
product competitiveness — one of our key priorities — and building         關鍵優先事項之一）及構建以效率為本的供應鏈
an efficiency-focused supply chain platform, without being swayed          平台，不受短期波動所影響。透過致力以穩定方
by short-term fluctuations. By striving to provide this essential food     式提供此等基本食品供應，本人將繼續為所有持
supply in a stable manner, I will continue to deliver long-term value to   份者（包括股東及投資者）創造長期價值。
all stakeholders, including shareholders and investors.


Grounded in our founder, Mr. Momofuku Ando’s philosophy, “Creating         秉承創辦人安藤百福先生「食創為世」之理念，
food to serve society” ( 食創為世 ), we remain committed to enriching          我們繼續致力透過提供美味、安全且隨手可得的
people’s lives by providing tasty, safe, and accessible food that          食品，讓消費者能夠享用有信譽的美食，從而豐
consumers can trust and enjoy.                                             富大眾的日常生活。




株主の皆様へ
日清食品有限公司（以下「当社」）董事会（以下「董事会」）を代表し、当社および子会社（以下「グループ」）の 2025 年度の年
次報告書をお届けいたします。


創業者       安藤百福氏の「食為聖職（食の仕事は聖職である）」という理念を基に、当社グループは「安全で信頼できる食」を
提供することに取り組んでまいりました。食品需要は、経済環境の変化にかかわらず成長基調にあります。当社グループ
はこの長期的なトレンドに沿 って成長を実現してまいりました。


2025 年度は、香港および中国本土の経済が外需に支えられて推移した一方、内需の回復は緩やかにとどまり、消費行動は
より選別的な状況となっています。このような環境下、当社グループは、オペレーション効率の向上とコスト管理の徹底
により、収益構造の改善を進めてまいりました。さらに、中長期的な成長に向けて「商品力の強化」および「サプライチ
ェーン・マネジメント（SCM）の高度化」を推進し、事業基盤の強化を着実に進めております。これらの取り組みの成果が
2025 年度の連結業績にも反映されました。


2025 年度の売上収益は、即席めんの販売数量の増加に加え、新たに買収した事業の貢献により、前年比 5.0％増の 4,001.1
百万香港ドルとなりました。売上総利益も前年比 5.6％増の 1,385.1 百万香港ドルとなり、売上総利益率は 34.6％と、前
年比 0.2％ポイントの改善になりました。




                                                                                 ANNUAL REPOR T 2025   二零二五年年報   09
     CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT 董事長報告

     営業利益は、前年比 8.1％増の 451.0 百万香港ドルとなり、営業利益率は 0.4％ポイント増加し 11.3％となりました。ま
     た、当社株主に帰属する利益は、前期に計上した減損損失が当期にはなく、前年比 64.9％増の 331.4 百万香港ドルとな
     り、利益率は 3.0％ポイント改善して 8.3％になりました。この結果、1 株当たり利益（”EPS”）は前年比 64.9％増の 31.76
     香港セントとなりました。売上拡大に加え、前期の一過性要因が解消されたことにより、粗利益、営業利益、最終利益段
     階のすべてのマージンが改善し収益性が底上げされました。


     当社グループは、安定的かつ持続的な株主還元を重要な配当方針と位置付けています。当期配当は、1 株当たり 15.88 香港
     セント（2024 年度：15.82 香港セント）で配当性向は 50.0％とする予定です。今後も当社グループは、安定したキャッシュ
     創出力を基盤としながら、成長投資と株主還元の適切なバランスを維持してまいります。


     設備投資
     業績の改善および株主還元と並行して、当社グループは、中長期的な成長基盤の強化を目的とした段階的な設備投資を継
     続しています。2025 年度には、将来の生産能力強化を見据えた戦略的投資として、中国・珠海市金湾区において、工業用
     地を総額 30.68 百万人民元で取得しました。


     当該用地では、2029 年の竣工を目標に新工場の建設を計画しており、現在、生産ラインの構成や生産計画を含む設備投資
     計画について、段階的な実施を前提とした検討を進めています。過去の工場建設および立ち上げ・運営を通じて培 った知
     見を活かし、生産ラインの高速化・自動化・省人化を推進してまいります。あわせて、中国の IT 力を活かすとともに、日
     本の品質管理の知見を取り入れながら、生産コストの低減と全社レベルでの生産効率向上を図ります。さらに、近隣に立
     地する珠海日清包装との連携を強化し、調達から生産・包装までを見通したサプライチェーン全体の効率向上を目指して
     まいります。


     リスク管理
     当社グループは、食の安全と安心を経営の中心に据え、食品安全および品質管理を最優先事項としています。すべての食
     品の生産拠点において ISO22000、FSSC22000、HACCP などの食品安全マネジメントの国際認証を取得するとともに、日清食
     品 HD グループの上海安全研究所と連携した定期的な品質監査およびサプライヤー監査を実施しています。主要原材料につ
     いては、複数サプライヤーによる調達体制を構築し、適切な在庫管理や長期契約を通じて、サプライチェーンリスクの低
     減に努めています。


     また、自然災害や感染症の流行といった事業継続リスクに備え、複数の拠点・地域分散型の生産体制を活かし、安定供給
     体制の維持を図 っています。


     食品安全と人材育成
     食品安全および品質管理を日常業務の中で確実に実践するためには、制度や仕組みの整備に加え、それを担う人材の育成
     が不可欠です。食品安全は、最終的には現場の従業員一人ひとりの判断と行動によって支えられるものであり、当社グル
     ープでは、IT を用いた継続的な教育を通じて、その考え方を組織全体に浸透させています。理念や行動規範に関するオン
     ライン学習に加え、ISO22000、FSSC22000、HACCP などの国際基準に準拠した食品安全研修を体系的に実施しています。


     さらに、衛生管理責任者や管理職、全従業員を対象に、衛生管理プログラムや内部監査員研修や外部機関による内部監査
     を年間計画に基づき継続的に実施し、現場対応力と判断力の向上を図 っています。




10   N I S S I N F O O D S CO M PA N Y L I M I T E D   日清食品有限公司
2026 年度の見通し
2026 年度に向けて、当社グループは外部環境の不確実性を注視しつつも、長期的な成長見通しについては引き続き前向
きに捉えています。コスト管理やオペレーション効率の向上に引き続き取り組みながら、重点テーマである「商品力の強
化」と「サプライチェーン・マネジメントの高度化」を進め、各市場における持続的な成長の実現を目指します。


中国本土では、生誕 55 周年を迎える「カップヌードル（合味道）」や「出前一丁」をはじめとする主力ブランドの高付加価
値商品を成長の軸とし、加えて縦型カップうどんの「日清どん兵衛」などの新商品を通じて商品ポートフォリオの拡充を
図ります。これにより、既存エリアでの販売強化や新たな販売チャネルの開拓を進めてまいります。


       「出前一丁」に加え、プレミアム版である「北海道一丁」や日本版「カップヌードル」などの販売拡大に取
香港においては、
り組むとともに、カップめんおよび袋めんの輸出を含めた売上拡大を図り、あわせて冷凍食品事業の回復に注力します。


その他地域市場においても、それぞれの市場特性を踏まえ、即席めんをはじめとした主力商品の販売拡大と市場基盤の強
化を進めます。


結び
香港に本社を置き、香港、中国本土およびその他地域市場で事業を展開する食品メーカーとして、当社グループは地域社
会や人 々の暮らしを支える責任を担 っています。董事長兼 CEO として、短期的な変動に左右されることなく、重点テーマ
である商品力の強化と、効率性に重点を置いたサプライチェーン基盤の構築に取り組んでまいります。社会にとって不可
欠な食を今後も安定的に提供し、株主・投資家を含むすべてのステークホルダーに対し、長期的な価値をお届けしていく
所存です。


創業者    安藤百福氏の「食創為世（食を通じて社会に貢献する）」という理念を基に、美味しく、安全で、消費者の皆様の
手に取りやすい食品を通じて、人 々の暮らしをより豊かにしてまいります。




Kiyotaka Ando                       安藤清隆
Chairman                            董事長

Risk Management and Internal Control                                            風險管理及內部監控
The Group has formulated and adopted the risk management policy in              本集團已制定及採納風險管理政策以為識別、評
providing direction in identifying, evaluating and managing significant         估及管理主要風險提供指導。主要風險管理程序
risks. The main features of the risk management processes comprise 5            包括五個核心流程：(a) 風險確定；(b) 風險評估及
core stages: (a) risk identification, (b) risk assessment and prioritisation,   其風險優先次序；(c) 風險應對；(d) 風險監控；及
(c) risk response, (d) risk monitoring and (e) risk reporting.                  (e) 風險匯報。

The appropriate risk mitigation plans are determined based on the               本集團根據以下風險應對措施釐定適當的風險減
following risk responses:                                                       緩計劃：

–     Acceptance: Risks are considered immaterial and acceptable                －   接受：根據本集團之風險接受程度，風險
      based on Group’s risk appetite, and therefore no action is                    被視為並不重大及可接受，故毋須採取任
      considered necessary.                                                         何行動。




                                                                                       ANNUAL REPOR T 2025   二零二五年年報   51
     CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT 企業管治報告

     –       Reduction: Risks cannot be considered immaterial, and actions           －   降低：風險被視為不能忽視，並採取監控
             such as controls have to be taken to reduce the impact and                  措施等行動以減少影響及風險承受程度至
             vulnerability to an acceptable level.                                       可接受水平。

     –       Sharing: Risks cannot be considered immaterial, and the Group           －   分擔：風險被視為不能忽視，本集團本身
             itself cannot effectively reduce the risks to an acceptably low             無法自行將風險有效降至可接受的水平，
             level, therefore a portion of the risks has to be transferred to or         故須透過保險及外判等方式將部分風險轉
             shared with other parties by insurance, outsourcing, etc.                   移至或與其他各方共同分擔。

     –       Avoidance: Risks are so significant that there is no measure,           －   防範：風險被視為相當重大，本集團沒有
             both internally and externally, to reduce the risk to an                    足夠及有效的內部監管系統及外界措施可
             acceptable level or involve unreasonably high costs to reduce               將風險降至可接受水平或需不合理及高昂
             the risk to an acceptable level. Therefore, activities giving rise to       之成本將風險降至可接受水平。因此，須
             risks should be avoided.                                                    避開產生有關風險之活動。

     The Group has adopted the COSO ERM Framework, the “Top-Down”                    本集團已採納COSO的風險管理框架（「由上而下」

     Taskforce and various departments within the Group.                             構。

     DIVIDEND                                                                    股息
     The Company considers stable and sustainable returns to the                 本公司將為股東帶來穩定及可持續回報視作其目
     shareholders to be its goal and endeavours to maintain a dividend           標及致力於維持可取得有關目標的股息政策。
     policy to achieve such a goal.

     The Board has adopted a dividend policy with effect from 1 January          董事會已採納於 2019 年 1 月 1 日起生效的股息政
     2019, which provides the guiding principles and procedures for              策，該政策規定有關派付股息之決策之指引原則
     making decisions on dividend payment. In deciding whether to                及程序。在決定是否建議宣派股息及釐定股息的
     propose dividends, and in determining the dividend amount, the              金額時，董事會應考慮（其中包括）：(i) 本集團的
     Board shall take into account, inter alia: (i) the Group’s unappropriated   未分配溢利╱盈利及對本集團長遠盈利能力之影
     profits/ earnings and the impact on the Group’s long-term earning           響；(ii) 本集團經營業績、盈利表現、現金流量、
     capacity; (ii) the Group’s results of operations, earnings performance,     財務狀況、未來前景、有關派付股息之法定及監
     cashflows, financial condition, future prospects, statutory and             管限制；及 (iii) 董事會認為適當之任何其他因素。
     regulatory restrictions on the payment of dividends; and (iii) any other
     factors that the Board considers relevant.

     The payment of dividend by the Company is also subject to any               本公司派付股息亦須遵守組織章程細則項下之任

     SHARE AWARD SCHEME                                                           股份獎勵計劃
     Purpose                                                                      目的
     A share award scheme (the “Share Award Scheme” or the “Scheme”)              本公司於 2016 年 3 月 7 日（「採納日期」）採納股
     was adopted by the Company on 7 March 2016 (the “Adoption                    份獎勵計劃（「股份獎勵計劃」或「該計劃」），並
     Date”) and amended on 26 March 2024. On 6 March 2026, the Board              於 2024 年 3 月 26 日經修訂。於 2026 年 3 月 6 日，
     resolved to renew the Scheme for a further term of 10 years from 7           董事會決議將該計劃續期十年，由 2026 年 3 月 7
     March 2026 (the “Amendment Date”) to 6 March 2036. The purpose               日（「 修 訂 日 期」）延 長 至 2036 年 3 月 6 日。 股 份
     of the Share Award Scheme is to recognise the contribution of certain        獎勵計劃的目的為認可部分經選定僱員（包括但
     selected employees (including but not limited to directors, executives,      不限於本集團任何成員公司的董事、行政人員、
     officers and other employees, whether full-time or part-time, of any         高級職員及其他僱員（全職或兼職））的貢獻，並
     members of the Group) and to provide the employees with incentives           向僱員給予獎勵，以挽留彼等為本集團的持續營
     in order to retain them for the continual operation and development          運及發展效力。
     of the Group.

     Eligible participants                                                        合資格參與者
     Participants include any employee, including without limitation,             參與者包括任何僱員，包括但不限於本集團任何
     the director (including former director), executive, officer and other       成員公司不時的董事（包括前董事）、行政人員、
     employees, whether full-time or part-time, of any member of the              高級職員及其他僱員（全職或兼職）。
     Group from time to time.

     Term                                                                         期限
     Subject to any early termination as may be determined by the Board           除非受董事會根據該計劃規則提出任何提前終止
     pursuant to the Scheme Rules, the Share Award Scheme shall be                所限，股份獎勵計劃於採納日期起計 10 年期間有
     valid and effective from the Adoption Date for a term of 10 years            效及生效，並於 2026 年 3 月 6 日屆滿。該計劃已
     and expired on 6 March 2026. The Scheme was renewed for a period             續期，由2026年3月7日至2036年3月6日為止。
     of 10 years from 7 March 2026 to 6 March 2036. The remaining life            截至本年報日期，該計劃的剩餘年限約為 9 年 11
     of Scheme is approximately 9 years 11 months as at the date of this          個月。
     annual report.

     Grant and acceptance                                                         授出及接受
     Subject to the terms of the Scheme and the Listing Rules, the Board          除非受該計劃的條款及上市規則的規限，董事會
     may, from time to time, grant awards to any eligible participant who         可不時向其認為已或將為本集團作出貢獻之任何
     the Board considers to have contributed or will contribute to the            合資格參與者授出獎勵。
     Group.

     To satisfy the awards, the Company may transfer to the trustee the           為落實獎勵，本公司應向受託人劃撥所需資金，
     necessary funds and instruct the trustee to acquire shares through on-       並指示受託人按當時市價透過場內交易收購股
     market transactions at the prevailing market price.                          份。

     employee at no cost to such selected employee on the vesting date            收入應於歸屬日期以零成本歸屬於有關經選定僱

     (a)     fulfilment of such vesting conditions (if any) as specified by the   (a)   達成本公司向經選定僱員發出的要約函中
             Board in the offer letter issued by the Company to the selected            董事會指定的有關歸屬條件（如有），且令
             employee up to the reasonable satisfaction of the Board;                   董事會合理滿意；

PERMITTED INDEMNITY PROVISION                                               獲准許之彌償條文
Pursuant to the Articles of Association, every director shall be entitled   根據組織章程細則並在公司條例准許之情況下，
to the indemnity out of the assets of the Company against any liability     每名董事均有權就其涉及本公司之任何民事或刑
incurred by him/her in relation to the Company in defending any             事訴訟進行抗辯而招致之任何負債，從本公司資
proceedings, whether civil or criminal, to the extent permitted by the      產中獲得彌償。有關獲准許之彌償條文已於截至
Companies Ordinance. Such permitted indemnity provision was in              2025 年 12 月 31 日止年度生效，並於本年報日期
force during the year ended 31 December 2025 and remains in force           一直生效。
at the date of this annual report.


REMUNERATION POLICY                                                         薪酬政策
The directors’ remuneration policy of the Company is decided by             本公司董事薪酬政策由薪酬委員會考慮到本公司
the Remuneration Committee, having regard to the Company’s                  之表現、董事的資歷及經驗和現行市場慣例而決
performance, qualification and experience of directors concerned and        定。
prevailing market practices.


SUFFICIENCY OF PUBLIC FLOAT                                                 足夠公眾持股量
Based on information that is publicly available to the Company and to       根據本公司獲得之公開資料以及就董事所深知，
the best knowledge of the Directors, the Board confirms that at the         董事會確認，於本年報日期，本公司維持上市規
date of this annual report, the Company has maintained a sufficient         則規定之足夠公眾持股量。
public float as required under the Listing Rules.


CHARITABLE DONATIONS                                                        慈善捐款
During the year ended 31 December 2025, the Group did not make              截至 2025 年 12 月 31 日止年度，本集團並無作出
any charitable or other donation.                                           任何慈善或其他捐款。


ESG                                                                         ESG
The Group has policies to promote environmental protection. In              本集團有推行環保的政策。為了在其每個業務階
order to reduce environmental impacts at every stage of its business        段減低對環境的影響，本集團採納大量政策監控
activities, the Group adopts a wide range of policies to control            污染物排放，鼓勵循環再用辦公室供應品，使用
pollutant emissions, encourage recycling of office supplies, use water      節水設施，以及確保遵循中港兩地之環境相關法
saving facilities, and ensure compliance with environmental laws and        律及法規。
regulations both in Hong Kong and the Chinese Mainland.

The ESG Report covering the same period is available together with          同 期 間 的 ESG 報 告 連 同 本 年 報 可 在 本 公 司 網 站
this annual report on the websites of both the Company (www.                (www.nissingroup.com.hk) 及 聯 交 所 網 站 查 閱
nissingroup.com.hk) and the Stock Exchange (www.hkexnews.hk).               (www.hkexnews.hk)。

     whole to purchase the finished goods from the Nissin Japan Group for
     its onward sale to distributors in the greater China region whom it has
     built a long term relationship with.

     Contractual cash flows are inconsistent with a basic lending              量，則與基本借貸安排不一致。該等修訂
     arrangement if they are indexed to a variable that is not a basic         指出，在某些情況下，或有特徵可能會在

     a contingent feature may give rise to contractual cash flows that         排一致的合約現金流量，但或有事項本身

                       Limited and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


                       The directors of the Company have, at the time of                   本公司董事於批准綜合財務報表時合
                       approving the consolidated financial statements, a                  理預期，本集團有充足資源於可預見
                       reasonable expectation that the Group has adequate                  未來持續經營。因此，彼等繼續採用
                       resources to continue in operational existence for the              持續經營基準會計處理方法編製綜合
                       foreseeable future. Thus, they continue to adopt the                財務報表。
                       going concern basis of accounting in preparing the
                       consolidated financial statements.

                      Basis of consolidation                                                    綜合基準
                       The consolidated financial statements incorporate                        綜合財務報表包括本公司以及由本公
                       the financial statements of the Company and entities                     司控制之實體及其附屬公司之財務報
                       controlled by the Company and its subsidiaries. Control is               表。當本公司滿足以下要素即擁有控
                       achieved when the Company:                                               制權：


                       •       has power over the investee;                                     •    對投資對象之權力；


                       •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its          •    對所參與投資對象之可變回報
                               involvement with the investee; and                                    承擔風險或擁有權利；及


                       •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.          •    有能力利用其權力以影響其回
                                                                                                     報。


                       The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an                       倘有事實及情況顯示上述三項控制權
                       investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there are              要素有一項或以上出現變動，本集團
                       changes to one or more of the three elements of control                  會重新評估其是否對投資對象擁有控
                       listed above.                                                            制權。


                       Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Company                    本公司獲得附屬公司控制權時將附屬
                       obtains control over the subsidiary and ceases when the                  公司綜合入賬，並於失去附屬公司控
                       Company loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically,                   制權時終止綜合入賬。具體而言，於
                       income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed                 年內收購或出售附屬公司之收入及開
                       of during the year are included in the consolidated                      支，按自本公司獲得控制權當日起至
                       statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive                      本集團不再控制附屬公司當日止，計
                       income from the date the Company gains control until the                 入綜合損益及其他全面收入表內。
                       date when the Company ceases to control the subsidiary.


                       Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive                 損益及其他全面收入之各組成部份乃
                       income are attributed to the owners of the Company                       歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控制性權
                       and to the non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive                益。附屬公司之全面收入額總歸屬於
                       income of the subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of                本公司擁有人及非控制性權益，即使
                       the Company and to the non-controlling interests even if                 此舉會導致非控制性權益產生虧絀結
                       this results in the non-controlling interests having a deficit           餘。
                       balance.


                       When necessary, adjustments are made to the financial                    附屬公司財務報表於必要時作出調

                       policies in line with the Group’s accounting policies.                   之會計政策一致。

           expenses and cash flows relating to transactions                             所有集團內部公司間之資產及負債、
           between members of the Group are eliminated in full on                       權益、收入、開支以及現金流量均於
           consolidation.                                                               綜合入賬時全數對銷。

           separately from the Group’s equity therein, which                            附 屬 公 司 中 權 益 分 開 呈 列， 其 指 現
           represent present ownership interests entitling their                        時擁有的權益且賦予持有人權利於清
           holders to a proportionate share of net assets of the                        盤時按比例分佔相關附屬公司資產淨
           relevant subsidiaries upon liquidation.                                      值。


           Business combinations                                                        業務合併
           A business is an integrated set of activities and assets                     業務為一組整合的活動及資產，當中
           which includes an input and a substantive process                            包括一項資源投入及一項實質過程，
           that together significantly contribute to the ability to                     而兩者必須對創造產出的能力有重大
           create outputs. The acquired processes are considered                        貢獻。倘收購過程對繼續生產產出的
           substantive if they are critical to the ability to continue                  能力至關重要，包括具備執行相關過
           producing outputs, including an organised workforce with                     程所需技能、知識或經驗的組織勞動
           the necessary skills, knowledge, or experience to perform                    力，或對持續生產產出的能力有重大
           the related processes or they significantly contribute to the                貢獻，則被視為實質性，以及被認為
           ability to continue producing outputs and are considered                     屬獨特或稀缺，或在無重大成本、努
           unique or scarce or cannot be replaced without significant                   力或持續生產產出能力出現延遲的情
           cost, effort, or delay in the ability to continue producing                  況下不可取代。
           outputs.


           Acquisitions of businesses, other than business                              除共同控制下的業務合併外，業務收
           combination under common control are accounted                               購使用收購法列賬。業務合併中轉讓
           for using the acquisition method. The consideration                          的代價按公平值計量，而公平值乃按
           transferred in a business combination is measured at fair                    本集團對被收購方的前擁有人所轉讓
           value, which is calculated as the sum of the acquisition-                    資產、承擔的負債及本集團為換取被
           date fair values of the assets transferred by the Group,                     收購方控制權所發行股本權益於收購
           liabilities incurred by the Group to the former owners of                    日期的公平值總和計算。收購相關成
           the acquiree and the equity interests issued by the Group                    本一般在產生時於損益確認。
           in exchange for control of the acquiree. Acquisition-
           related costs are generally recognised in profit or loss as
           incurred.

           Goodwill                                                                     商譽
           Goodwill arising on an acquisition of a business is carried                  收購業務產生之商譽按收購業務日期
           at cost as established at the date of acquisition of the                     之成本減累計減值虧損（如有）列賬。
           business less accumulated impairment losses, if any.


           For the purposes of impairment testing, goodwill is                          就減值測試而言，商譽會分配至預期
           allocated to each of the Group’s cash-generating units (or                   可從合併的協同效益中獲益的本集團
           group of cash-generating units) that is expected to benefit                  各現金產生單位（或一組現金產生單
           from the synergies of the combination, which represent                       位），即為內部管理目的而監控商譽
           the lowest level at which the goodwill is monitored for                      的最低水平，且不大於經營分部。
           internal management purposes and is not larger than an
           operating segment.


           A cash-generating unit (or group of cash-generating                          獲分配商譽的現金產生單位（或一組
           units) to which goodwill has been allocated is tested                        現金產生單位）每年進行減值測試，
           for impairment annually or more frequently when                              或於有跡象顯示該單位可能出現減值
           there is indication that the unit may be impaired. If the                    時 更 頻 繁 地 進 行 減 值 測 試。 倘 可 收
           recoverable amount is less than its carrying amount, the                     回金額低於其賬面值，則減值虧損首
           impairment loss is allocated first to reduce the carrying                    先獲分配以減少任何商譽的賬面值，
           amount of any goodwill and then to the other assets on a                     然後根據該單位（或一組現金產生單
           pro-rata basis based on the carrying amount of each asset                    位）內各資產的賬面值按比例分配至
           in the unit (or group of cash-generating units).                             其他資產。

           employees.

                       Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from                     全期預期信貸虧損指相關工具於預計
                       all possible default events over the expected life of the                 全期內所有可能違約事件所產生的預
                       relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                      期 信 貸 虧 損。 相 反，12 個 月 預 期 信
                       ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                      貸虧損（「12 個月預期信貸虧損」）指
                       expected to result from default events that are possible                  預計於報告日期後 12 個月內可能發
                       within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment                     生的違約事件產生的部分全期預期信
                       are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                      貸虧損。評估乃根據本集團過往信貸
                       experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the                 虧損經驗，並就債務人特定因素、一
                       debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                    般經濟狀況以及對於報告日期之過往
                       of past event and current conditions at the reporting date                事件及當時狀況及未來經濟狀況預測
                       as well as the forecast of future economic conditions.                    的評估作調整。


                       The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                        本集團一直確認貿易應收賬款之全期
                       receivables. The ECL on these assets are assessed                         預期信貸虧損。該等資產的預期信貸
                       individually for debtors.                                                 虧損乃就債務人進行個別評估。


                       For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                    就所有其他工具而言，本集團計量虧
                       allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been                    損撥備等於 12 個月預期信貸虧損，
                       a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          除非當信貸風險自初始確認以來顯著
                       the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The assessment of                      上升，則本集團確認全期預期信貸虧
                       whether lifetime ECL should be recognised is based on                     損。評估是否應確認全期預期信貸虧
                       significant increases in the likelihood or risk of a default              損乃根據自初始確認以來發生違約的
                       occurring since initial recognition.                                      可能性或風險顯著上升。


                       Derecognition of financial assets                                         終止確認金融資產
                       The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the                    僅當資產現金流量之合約權利屆滿
                       contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire,               時，或其將金融資產以及資產擁有權
                       or when it transfers the financial asset and substantially                之絕大部分風險及回報轉移予另一實
                       all the risks and rewards of ownership of the asset to                    體時，本集團方會終止確認該金融資
                       another entity.                                                           產。


                       On derecognition of a financial asset measured at                         於終止確認按攤銷成本計量之金融資
                       amortised cost, the difference between the asset’s carrying               產時，資產賬面值與已收及應收代價
                       amount and the sum of the consideration received and                      間之差額於損益內確認。
                       receivable is recognised in profit or loss.

      4.      KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION UNCERTAINTY                                 4.   估計不明朗因素之重要來源
              In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are           於應用本集團之會計政策時（詳述於附註
              described in note 3, the directors of the Company are required             3)，本公司董事須對未能透過其他來源確
              to make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the                    定之資產及負債賬面值作出判斷、估計及
              carrying amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily            假設。所作出之估計及相關假設乃以過往
              apparent from other sources. The estimates and underlying                  經驗及其他被視為相關之因素為基準。實
              assumptions are based on historical experience and other                   際業績可能與該等估計有異。
              factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual results may
              differ from these estimates.


              The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on                   估計及相關假計乃按持續基準審閱。倘對
              an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are                   會計估計之修訂僅影響進行修訂之期間，
              recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if the           則於該期間確認，或倘修訂會影響目前及
              revision affects only that period, or in the period of the revision        未來期間，則會於修訂及未來期間確認。
              and future periods if the revision affects both current and future
              periods.

              a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of the assets and            負債賬面值作出重大調整。
              liabilities within the next financial year.


              Estimated impairment of goodwill                                           商譽之估計減值
              Determining whether goodwill is impaired requires an                       釐定商譽有否減值須估計獲分配商譽之現
              estimation of the recoverable amount of the cash-generating                金產生單位（或現金產生單位組別）之可收
              unit (or group of cash-generating units) to which goodwill has             回金額，即使用價值或公平值減出售成本
              been allocated, which is the higher of the value in use or fair            之較高者。計算使用價值須本集團估計預
              value less costs of disposal. The value in use calculation requires        期現金產生單位（或現金產生單位組別）將
              the Group to estimate the future cash flows expected to arise              產生之未來現金流量及合適折現率，以計
              from the cash-generating unit (or a group of cash-generating               算現值。倘實際未來現金流量低於預期，
              units) and a suitable discount rate in order to calculate the              或事實及情況有變導致下調未來現金流量
              present value. Where the actual future cash flows are less than            或上調貼現率，則可能出現重大減值虧損
              expected, or change in facts and circumstances which results in            或進一步減值虧損。
              downward revision of future cash flows or upward revision of
              discount rate, a material impairment loss or further impairment
              loss may arise.

      6.      SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                 6.   分部資料
              The Group is organised into operating business units according           本集團根據相關集團實體之主要經營地點
              to the major place of operations of the relevant group entities.         組成營運業務單位。本集團參考其各自之
              The Group determines its operating segments based on these               主要經營地點根據業務單位釐定其營運分
              business units by reference to their respective major place of           部，並將資料呈報主要經營決策者，即本
              operations, for the purpose of reporting to the chief operating          公司董事總經理。
              decision maker, i.e. the managing director of the Company.


              During the year ended 31 December 2024, the Group had two                於 截 至 2024 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度，本 集 團
              operating segments, namely Hong Kong and other regions,                  共有兩個營運分部，即香港及其他地區與
              and Chinese Mainland. As at 1 January 2025, the Group has                中 國 內 地。 於 2025 年 1 月 1 日， 本 集 團
              separately accounted for the costs associated with the group             將總部集中管理的集團活動的相關成本單
              activities that are managed centrally at head office to provide a        獨入賬，以提供更相關的分部呈列，並據
              more relevant segment presentation and re-defined its operating          此重新定義其營運分部，即香港及其他地
              segments, namely Hong Kong and other regions, Chinese                    區、中國內地及總部。
              Mainland and Head office accordingly.

17. RIGHT-OF-USE ASSETS (continued)                                         17. 使用權資產（續）
     For both years, the Group leases office premises and staff               本集團於兩年內租賃辦公室物業及員工宿
     quarters for its operations. Lease contracts are entered into for        舍以供其營運。租賃合約乃按固定年期一
     fixed terms of one to five years (2024: one to three years), but         至 五 年（2024 年： 一 至 三 年）訂 立， 惟 可
     may have extension and termination options. Lease terms are              選擇延期及終止。租期乃在個別基礎上協
     negotiated on an individual basis and contain different terms            商，並包含不同的條款及條件。於釐定租
     and conditions. In determining the lease term and assessing              期及評估不可撤回期間的長度時，本集團
     the length of the non-cancellable period, the Group applies the          應用合同的定義並釐定合同可強制執行的
     definition of a contract and determines the period for which the         期間。
     contract is enforceable.


     In addition, the Group owns several industrial buildings where           此外，本集團擁有數座主要位於其製造設
     its manufacturing facilities are primarily located. The Group is         施內的工業樓宇。本集團為該等物業權益

      35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                   35. 資本風險管理
              The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the            本集團之資本管理旨在確保本集團旗下實
              Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                 體將能持續經營，同時透過經營產生之溢
              maximising the return to shareholders through the generation            利為利益相關方爭取最高回報。本集團之
              of profits from its operations. The Group’s overall strategy            整體策略較過往年度維持不變。
              remains unchanged from prior year.


              The capital structure of the Group consists of cash and cash            本集團資本結構包括現金及現金等價物及
              equivalents and equity attributable to owners of the Company,           本公司擁有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本及
              comprising issued share capital and reserves.                           儲備）。


              The directors of the Company review the capital structure on an         本公司董事按年度基準審閱資本結構。作
              annual basis. As part of this review, the directors consider the        為此審閱之一環，董事考慮資本成本及與
              cost of capital and the risks associated with its capital. Based on     資本相關之風險。本集團將根據董事之意
              recommendations of the directors, the Group will balance its            見透過支付股息及發行新股來平衡其整體
              overall capital structure through the payment of dividends and          資本結構。
              new share issues.

           (iii) Other price risk                                                 (iii) 其他價格風險
                The Group is exposed to equity price risk through                      本集團因按公平值計入損益計
                its investments in listed equity securities measured                   量之上市股本證券投資而面對
                at FVTPL. The Group is also exposed to certain price                   股本價格風險。本集團亦面對
                risks in relation to unquoted equity securities for                    若干與長期策略而於被投資公
                investees for long term strategic purposes which                       司的無報價股本證券有關的價
                had been measured at FVTPL. The Group monitors                         格風險，該股本證券按公平值
                the price risk through regular reviews of the market                   計入損益計量。本集團通過定
                price of the listed equity securities and will consider                期檢討上市股本證券的市價監

     As part of the Group’s credit risk management, the Group uses                      本集團信貸風險管理的其中一環是本

     terms.

     are adjusted for forward-looking information that is available                     就不過多投入資金或精力即可獲得的
     without undue cost or effort. The grouping is regularly reviewed                   前瞻性資料而作調整。組別劃分由管

              (c)      Fair value measurements of financial instruments                          (c)        金融工具公平值計量
                       Certain of the Group’s financial instruments are measured                            本集團若干金融工具就財務呈報目的
                       at fair value for financial reporting purposes. The                                  按公平值計量。管理層將就公平值計
                       management is to determine the appropriate valuation                                 量釐定適合估值技術及輸入數據。
                       techniques and inputs for fair value measurements.


                       In estimating the fair value, the Group uses market-                                 於估計公平值時，本集團採用其所得
                       observable data to the extent it is available. For                                   市場可觀察數據。就存在重大不可觀
                       instruments with significant unobservable inputs under                               察輸入數據的第三層級工具而言，本
                       Level 3, the Group engages third party qualified valuers                             集團委聘第三方合資格估值師進行估
                       to perform the valuation. The management works                                       值。管理層與合資格外聘估值師緊密
                       closely with the qualified external valuers to establish the                         合作設立模式適用之估值技術及輸入
                       appropriate valuation techniques and inputs to the model.                            數據。管理層定期向本公司董事會匯
                       The management reports the findings to the board of                                  報結果以解釋公平值波動的原因。
                       directors of the Company regularly to explain the cause of
                       fluctuations in the fair value.
